- Step 1 (Universe screen, upstream): 508 candidates cleared the full eligibility rubric — NYSE/AMEX/NASDAQ-listed stock, ADR, or ETF; no open-end mutual funds; no raw index tickers; price > $5.00; market cap > $2B for stocks (well above the $500M rubric floor, per the 'no small-cap' mandate) or AUM > $500M for ETFs.

- Step 5 (Diversification cap): max 2 new picks per sector, filling 0 of 0 open slot(s).
- Step 6 (Floor check): minimum basket size of 3 already met, no relaxation needed.
